NISSEQUOGUE RIVER NEAR SMITHTOWN NY, USGS site 01304000 (USGS-01304000), stream in Suffolk County, New York, draining 27 square miles. Daily means from 1943-10-01 to 2022-10-03, a page a water year (October to September), linked below. Discharge in cubic feet a second, gage height in feet above the gauge's datum. Its annual peaks are its floods; its field measurements are the visits that check the gauge. Revisions are USGS's own notes of the site's published history, as written.
